# 2026-10-17 源码审计整改与开发门禁

**当前目标与续做入口：** [可连续开发目标](DEVELOPMENT_GOALS_REVIEW_20261017.md)。本文件保留既有整改和实验结果，后续工作追加记录；下文“下一步”是当时记录，当前顺序与任务状态以最新目标为准。

依据用户提供的 `NautilusTrader-RL-ES_源码深度审计与后续开发建议_20261017.md` 核对当前工作树。该报告审查的是 GitHub 提交 `c24d1bc`；本工作树还包含 2026-10-16 开发中的未提交修改。以下状态以当前源码和本地测试为准。报告中的收益、吞吐及真实流动性推测只作为待验证假设。

## 本轮已落实

| 审计项 | 当前修改和证据 | 边界 |
|---|---|---|
| A08 | 同时间戳归并、事件 ID 幂等/冲突拒绝、平仓核对、单一 quote-settled 单位乘数线性持仓估值及原生线性强平逐事件权益核对均已实现。真实 8 小时 ETHUSDT golden 与冻结 Train-inner 90 天 long exposure 的已标记持仓屏障残差为 0；Engine 级线性强平小样例也残差为 0。 | 不含外部现金流；强平压力规则/费用为合成测试参数，不认证历史成本；逆向/多资产账户不适用。 |
| A01 | 新应用 E2 默认只搜索仓位缩放和回撤节流两维，避免两个常数相乘造成的冗余；旧三维 RunSpec 指纹与 Checkpoint 仍可恢复。精确 `(1,1)` 候选已经纳入真实评估、预算、选择和恢复；冻结 L4 actor + 合成 perpetual Funding trace parity 通过。 | 只认证实现等价，不认证市场表现。旧三维显式 schema 仅为恢复兼容。 |
| A02 | 应用配置新增版本选择。`legacy_weighted_v1` 仍为默认；`constraint_priority_train_inner_v1` 按原生 insolvency、已认证维持保证金和强平事实筛选，按 `tanh` 有界费用后经济分数排名；拒绝原因进入代际报告、检查点和 bundle lineage。三目标同 actor/场景/预算的 A2 搜索及 Train-inner 原生回放已完成。 | 实验未观察到不可行候选，候选筛选经验效果未在搜索中触发；scenario-only 规则且无 Validation 证据，不能宣称目标改进或策略盈利。 |
| A03 | 合成五步回归与 90 天 Train-inner T0/T1 原生回放均完成；R3 修复后，T1 90 个每日独立账户 lane 完整覆盖 129,597 个决策点，终止强平及费用、Funding、持仓时长和权益差均有报告。 | 固定目标、scenario-only 规则；T1 PnL 为独立账户和，不构成复利收益或强制平仓纯因果归因；T2 尚未开发。旧 r2 的 87 步缺口已由 R3 修正，历史数值仅保留作审计轨迹。 |
| A10 | Windows Job/Recovery 与本轮 QA 脚本使用运行时 Windows API 解析；Linux 平台 MyPy 对 121 个源码文件及 Windows 两模块 `--platform win32` 均通过。将 2024-01-01 的 1 秒采样 bookTicker 测试 Parquet、完成标记和公开来源摘要放入 `tests/fixtures/`；Quote 用例纳入默认 PR `micro_golden`。最近 Windows PR 选择 **478 passed、60 deselected、38 warnings**。 | 远程 Linux/Windows CI 尚未运行；长窗真实 golden 仍留在 nightly；历史规则仍是 scenario-only。 |
| A12 | M2.2 可选逐决策 trace 已把 decision/plan/idempotency IDs 关联到订单、fills、费用、终态与未成交原因；执行结果报告默认只给摘要，CLI `--execution-trace` 开启明细。 | 逐订单 trace 仅 opt-in；成本与流动性仍受当前 Bar 模型假设限制。 |
| A06 | 90 天 Train-inner 固定敞口 Bar Base/Stress 对照已报告订单量、成交量、订单/数量 fill ratio、滑点、费用、Funding、净收益和回撤。 | Bar 模拟足量深度；Quote/Depth 深度与真实历史流动性仍未认证。 |
| A05 | WFO DSR 输出新增试验数范围：当前冻结协议候选；项目历史试验数标为未估计。 | 不能把该 DSR 当作全历史多重检验校正。 |

先前工作树已有项目级 Train/正式区间访问登记（A04）、Base/Stress 成本情景（A06）、动作到数量取整/原生敞口的漏斗诊断（A12）和独立市场覆盖报告（A13）。这些功能的存在不构成 Holdout 尚未污染、真实盘口深度或费用后 Alpha 认证。

## C 阶段预注册 gamma 配对实验的结果

`configs/discount_half_life_probe_20261017.json` 冻结了同一安全 Train 区间、3 个相同 seed、每臂每 seed 32,768 步的控制组 `gamma=0.99` 与六小时半衰期组 `gamma=0.9980764435756287`。6 个训练运行完成。首次诊断窗口预检为 DQ-D，未执行模型诊断；随后在查看任何模型表现前冻结 `configs/discount_half_life_evaluation_20261017.json`，改用三个 DQ-B Train-inner 开发窗口。36 份原生模型报告完成，Base/Stress 中每个 seed 的三窗平均配对收益差均为 0；两个组在多数窗口空仓。预注册门禁 `FAIL_NO_CROSS_WINDOW_MECHANISM_ADVANTAGE`，`candidate_promotion=false`、`budget_expansion_allowed=false`。这只否定当前固定设置下的预算扩张，不证明其它动作契约或数据没有 Alpha。原始结果位于 `.artifacts/audit-development-20261016/gamma-half-life-eval-v1/summary.json`，六个训练包位于 `gamma-half-life-v5/`；两份计划及模型清单都有 SHA sidecar。

## 目标建立时记录的计划顺序（历史；当前续做见活动目标）

1. **经济目标门禁（A02/A11）**：在相同 Train-inner 场景和预算下，比较旧 Fitness、去 HardRisk 惩罚、先筛硬约束再按经济指标排序。加入原生亏损/空仓/盈利/违规四类确定性测试，并记录 E1→R1→E2 每阶段的原生成交、费用、Funding、收益和回撤差。现阶段不增加 ES 搜索预算。
2. **中性 E2 门禁（A01）**：把精确 R1 中性候选纳入有账本记录的候选矩阵和评价预算；逐步比对相同 Actor 的订单、fill、权益和终态。只有改进可在固定验证情景复现时才采纳 E2 参数。
3. **Episode 时域门禁（A03）**：同一固定策略做完整连续账户与等长强制清仓窗口配对，报告人为终止成交、费用、Funding 持有时长和权益差。采样边界不产生现金流的连续账户方案另立实验。
4. **执行与数据门禁（A06/A07/A10/A12）**：继续扩展 Actor→Soft→Hard→Planner→NativeFill 的逐决策归因；Quote/Depth 情景仅在有历史来源时启用。先基准 60/360 天 Reset，再考虑事件索引缓存。把可分发小样本与外部真实大文件测试分开，Linux/Windows CI 各自验证。
5. **研究统计与能力边界（A04/A05/A09/A14）**：项目级登记历史候选/seed/特征版本并冻结试验数估计政策；新独立样本准备就绪前，不重用已消费 Test/Holdout。BTC/SOL、Hedge Mode、>1 倍杠杆保持未认证；先完成当前 ETH NETTING 原生经济事实及简单基线的配对反事实。

每项实验须先冻结源码、数据快照、规则来源、参数、Seed、成本假设、主指标、停机线和可访问的数据分区。已有历史规则缺口仍使收益只代表明示情景，不能报告为真实可交易收益。

本轮验证：ES/应用/历史工厂/研究账本/Compute 定向回归 **142 passed**；在加入 Episode 与 Quote 夹具后，指标/历史工厂/真实黄金样例再回归 **52 passed**。Ruff `packages tests qa` 通过，Linux 平台 MyPy `packages qa` **121 source files** 通过；Windows API 两模块 Windows 平台 MyPy 通过。未将这些定向结果称作全量 CI 或真实策略盈利认证。最终源码快照 `.artifacts/audit-development-20261016/source-snapshot-v14.zip` 包含此前尚未提交工作及本轮可交付 Quote 夹具。

## 活动目标首轮实现：M0 账本、CI 基础与研究登记

用户随后明确要求创建 Codex 可自动实施的活动目标，已启用并授权连续开发，取代此前文档中的“等待启动”。本轮直接修改实现，没有新增正式评价或访问用户的 Test/Holdout；测试使用隔离研究登记。

### 账本修改与证据

- `native_metrics.py`：统一验证事件 ID、快照时间/币种、成交 BUY/SELL、正且有限的数量/价格及参考价；同 ID 的完全相同事实只计一次，矛盾记录明确失败，包含 Funding 跨时间戳冲突。
- 新增 `reconcile_linear_equity`，独立累积原生成交的带符号价格现金流、手续费与 Funding，在每个平仓屏障核对 `期初权益 + 成交价格盈亏 - 手续费 + Funding`。同时间戳决策与终止屏障分别核对；实际成交价已含滑点，不再次扣减。容差按显式每事件舍入上界乘以已观察事件数，不隐藏残差。
- 应用 `rollout` 接入该核对，完整报表保留逐屏障差额，摘要模式只保留统计。适用范围为无外部现金流、报价币结算、单位乘数的线性账户；**持仓屏障未做估值认证**，报告单列 `unchecked_open_barriers`；逆向合约、多资产和出入金不适用。
- 回归补了手算多/空两个 round trip、多 Funding、重复累计快照、同时间戳终止与篡改权益；净收益/手续费/Funding/滑点与独立金额核对。真实 ETHUSDT 场景黄金测试继续精确匹配既有原生终态权益 `9998.21338803`，并通过含 Funding 的终态独立核对。

### CI 基础缺口

- 新增 `.gitattributes`，对 `tests/fixtures/**` 禁用文本换行转换。当前仓库 `core.autocrlf=true`；本地 Git checkout-filter 对照证明：没有属性时规则来源 JSON 的 23 行变为 CRLF，SHA 为 `912b788c045c7a1ec0c28fff759537da9724330938c1f3a9be92dc9749a96f45`，与声明不符；有属性时恢复声明的 `b73028b1fca697c6dda6196a1b7c9e89d7aed0815ccb7d0facef900b3b67152f`。证据为 [m0_fixture_checkout.json](audit_evidence/20261017/m0_fixture_checkout.json)。这是本地过滤器复现，不是远程 CI 成功声明。
- `test_native_liquidation_facts.py` 移除 `runpy` 加载被忽略的本机上游 `examples/backtest/liquidation_demo.py`，改为使用已安装原生公开 API 的自包含报价/策略夹具。真实原生强平标记和 compact Position 字段等价测试通过；该 inverse fixture 只认证原生清算事实读取，不认证线性或高杠杆账户经济。
- 既有源码清单与打包器纳入 `.gitattributes`，打包器不再强行依赖上游示例。尚未提交/推送，未触发远程 Linux/Windows CI；Quote 夹具仍为已有未跟踪交付内容，后续源码交付必须纳入。

### 研究登记修复

- 原 SQL 对同 owner 豁免所有重叠，允许换 key 将已开发区间改称 Holdout，或重开原 Holdout。现在涉及 Holdout 的重叠即使 owner 相同也拒绝；完全相同 key/身份仍幂等，访问状态继续阻止重复 consume。
- 既有协议内 Test/development 的 owner 规则保留，以兼容顺序 WFO；不得将这一规则称为对任意调用者的安全隔离。旧历史记录允许记录已经存在的污染，但以 `recorded` 状态导入，不产生新评价授权。
- 历史清单的所有条目与导入完成标记在同一 SQLite 事务提交；中途非法条目会整体回滚。新增测试覆盖同 owner 的五种 Holdout 重标/重读组合、历史重叠的保守导入、导入幂等与部分失败回滚。

### 实际检查

运行前设置 `PYTHONPATH` 为所有工作区 `packages/*/src`，并确认 `rles_research.native_metrics.__file__` 指向当前源码，避免误测 `.venv` 内旧 wheel。测试解释器为 `.venv/Scripts/python.exe`（Python 3.14.7）。

```powershell
$env:PYTHONPATH = ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath packages -Directory | ForEach-Object { Join-Path $_.FullName 'src' }) -join [IO.Path]::PathSeparator)
.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest -q tests/test_research_metrics.py tests/test_native_liquidation_facts.py tests/test_historical_factory.py tests/test_real_golden_ethusdt.py tests/test_application_cli.py -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp .tmp/pytest-goal-accounting-v1-20261017 --tb=short
.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest -q tests/test_native_liquidation_facts.py tests/test_research_access.py tests/test_research_wfo.py -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp .tmp/pytest-goal-registry-v1-20261017 --tb=short
.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m ruff check packages tests qa ci
.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m mypy --platform linux packages qa
```

结果：账本/工厂/真实行情/应用定向 **94 passed、18 warnings、71.37 秒**；研究登记/WFO/自包含强平夹具 **63 passed、4.46 秒**；全项目 Ruff 通过，Linux 平台 MyPy **121 source files** 通过。初次只指定两个文件跑 MyPy 时导入解析指向未带类型标记的已装 wheel，出现 20 个 import-untyped；改为明确检查工作区 `packages qa` 后通过，未通过关闭诊断来规避。

完整 CPU 回归结果：**521 passed、4 GPU deselected、46 warnings、281.27 秒**，无失败。JUnit 位于 `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m0-cpu.xml`；已解析 XML 核实 521 个用例、0 failures/0 errors/0 skipped，小型证据为 [m0_verification.json](audit_evidence/20261017/m0_verification.json)。本轮新增的实现和测试保持冻结至全量检查结束；没有在运行中更改实现。该结果认证当前 Windows 源码环境的 CPU 回归，不等于远程 Linux/Windows CI、GPU 或收益认证。

```powershell
.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest -q tests -m 'not gpu' -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp .tmp/pytest-goal-m0-cpu-20261017 --tb=short --junitxml .artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m0-cpu.xml
```

当时 M1.1 已通过实现等价门禁，M1.2 目标实现和匹配预算 Train-inner 搜索已完成，独立 Validation 仍未完成；该段记录的游标为 M0.1。此后本计划追加了 M2.1、M2.2 和 M2.3 的实际结果；当前续做游标以 [当前开发目标](DEVELOPMENT_GOAL_CURRENT.md) 为准：M2.4（A07）。A01–A14 尚未全部闭环。

## 2026-10-17 M1.1 精确中性 E2 候选进入评估协议

- 目标：把物理 `1.0` 全维 R1 中性参数作为独立候选，与每代 CMA-ES 候选在相同 Train-inner 场景种子下真实运行；让请求预算、L6 登记、选择结果及检查点恢复都看见该候选。
- 实现：`GenerationLaneEvaluator` 对新 E2 协议额外派发 `neutral-r1`，为每代持久化得分与证据，并将中性物理向量和协议版本纳入新检查点签名；禁用该功能时保留既有 schema 3 签名，恢复旧 Checkpoint 不漂移。`ESStageExecutor` 对优化器冠军与中性候选按既有最高 fitness 规则比较，同分优先返回精确全 `1.0` 中性参数。`EvolutionResult` 和阶段使用量计入每代额外候选；应用新 E2 RunSpec 的 evaluation/lane cap 与每代多出的请求一致，并在 `run.json` 记录协议版本。旧 manifest 恢复旧 E2 上限与协议，避免给旧检查点补记未执行的评估。
- 回归：A4 真实 ES/RL/ES + Native Engine + SQLite L6 流程现在实际评估中性候选，并核对 lane/evaluation 使用量；另有同种子 CRN、预算/检查点恢复、精确中性选择及旧 E2 应用恢复回归。相关文件集回归 **99 passed、29 warnings、198.40 秒**；签名兼容修正后的 ES checkpoint/真实应用恢复专项 **17 passed、17 warnings、151.62 秒**；参数选择、评估器恢复与 A4 路由回归 **8 passed、14 warnings、14.30 秒**。新增固定 actor 的同种子合成原生 spot 回放，对比基准 SoftRisk 与精确 `(1,1)`：请求/调整目标、计划动作、实际敞口、逐步权益/收益/回撤、归一化 fills 与终态权益完全相同（**1 passed，1.26 秒**）。该夹具无 Funding，使用 canonical fixture actor，不能代替导出 L4 R1 actor + perpetual Funding 场景。全新 A4 恢复用例与新增中性选择 helper 已分别在专项覆盖。Ruff 通过；显式设置 `PYTHONPATH`/`MYPYPATH` 指向工作区后，MyPy 对 ES 核心与 Orchestrator ES/application 共 **4 source files** 通过。M0 时全工作区 Linux MyPy **121 files** 通过证据仍有效。
- 结果边界：本轮只使用合成 Train-inner perpetual episode，未读取市场样本或访问 Validation/Test/Holdout。先在同一 episode 训练并导出 PPO frozen L4 actor，再以相同 seed 分别使用基准 SoftRisk 和中性乘子 `(1,1)` 原生回放；逐决策风险目标、计划、执行暴露、权益/经济收益/回撤、归一化成交、两笔 Funding、账户行和最终指标完全一致。预算、真实 A4 账本计数与恢复回归一并通过，M1.1 标记 `PASS`（仅限实现等价，不含市场表现）。
- 下一步：进入 M1.2 约束优先适应度。原生回放目前可观察且可靠的候选级硬约束是终态破产（最终权益归零）；HardRisk 调整次数是干预诊断，不能充当违规计数；结算/清算证据缺失继续作为无效运行拒绝。新版本需有有界且确定性的经济排序、可审计拒绝原因和冻结配置身份，同时不改变旧检查点身份。

## 2026-10-17 M1.2 约束优先适应度与匹配预算 Train-inner 对照

- 目标：旧加权 `FitnessSpec` 作为可复现基线保留；新协议先筛硬约束，再按配置的费用后净回报、最差尾损和最大回撤排名，并在 Run 的配置/ES evaluator 身份中明确版本。
- 实现：新增 `ConstraintPriorityFitnessSpec` / `FitnessDecision`，协议名为 `constraint_priority_train_inner_v1`。`NautilusBarrierEnv` 每步暴露 solvency 及认证的原生 margin/liquidation 事实；L6 candidate session 跨步累计 episode insolvency、maintenance margin breach、native liquidation，并把事实加入证据摘要。约束候选分数固定为 `-2`，可行候选分数为配置化 `tanh` 费用后经济聚合值（严格位于 `(-1,1)`），HardRisk intervention rate 不进入新目标。破产、维持保证金不足、原生强平分别保留结构化拒绝原因。新协议遇到 margin 事实未知时失败关闭；结算/terminal drain 无法证明仍作为无效运行中止。配置 `es_fitness.protocol` 显式选协议，legacy 仍为默认；legacy evaluator identity 和调用参数序列不变，新协议版本隔离 checkpoint。
- 回归：公式级利润/空仓/亏损/破产、分数有界、HardRisk 干预中立、margin breach、原生强平和未知 margin 事实拒绝均有确定性覆盖；候选原因聚合、E2 检查点恢复及 A4 新协议恢复后的 bundle lineage 均通过。最新 M1.2 定向回归 **17 passed、13 warnings、31.26 秒**；Ruff touched source/tests 通过，MyPy 4 个核心源码文件通过，`git diff --check` 通过。
- 三目标 Train-inner 对照：固定 checksum-verified ETHUSDT 2024-01-04 归档夹具，使用同一个 frozen R1 actor、`seed=31`、共同 `scenario_fold_id=a02-three-fitness-crn-20261017`、相同两代 CMA-ES 配置（population 4、8 optimizer evaluations、每臂 10 lane requests，含每代 R1 neutral 候选）。legacy、零 HardRisk 干预惩罚、constraint-priority 三臂均先以 `train(evaluate=False)` 搜索，再只对同臂选中参数执行相同 Train-inner native replay。legacy 净收益 **-0.1578%**，费用/资金费/滑点分别 **12.2536 / 0.0160 / 2.5314**，换手率 **2.4528**；零惩罚与约束优先选中同一参数，二者净收益均 **-0.1510%**，成本分别 **11.4319 / 0.0147 / 2.3616**，换手率 **2.2882**。三臂均为 **0 infeasible candidates**，所以本次搜索没有经验触发约束筛选；单测覆盖了 margin/强平分类分支。证据：[m1_2_three_objective_train_inner.json](audit_evidence/20261017/m1_2_three_objective_train_inner.json)，SHA-256 `D4036715857AAF9BD97070E48C28B8FFBE97E5A3DF22BC1A0F7AC821CF0E363F`；复现脚本 [compare_es_fitness_objectives_20261017.py](../qa/compare_es_fitness_objectives_20261017.py)，完整运行产物位于 `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m12-three-objective/`。
- 边界与状态：这是一个 scenario-only 规则日窗、Train-inner 诊断，不读取 Validation/Test/Holdout。三目标分值使用不同尺度，不能跨协议比较 raw fitness；原生回放均亏损，不存在收益晋级结论。匹配预算搜索和候选级 margin/liquidation 事实分类已完成，M1.2 保持 `PARTIAL`，因为尚无冻结且可用的独立 Validation 证据。工程证据见 [m1_2_constraint_priority.json](audit_evidence/20261017/m1_2_constraint_priority.json)。
- 下一步：保留 M1.2 当前游标，等待/核实冻结协议允许的独立 Validation 和有日期证据的经济规则；此前可继续不依赖新样本的工程整改，但不得把这次 scenario-only 搜索解释为目标收益改善，不访问未获准的 Test/Holdout。

## 2026-10-17 M2.2 执行 trace 完成与 M2.1 T0/T1 Train-inner 对照

- M2.2 实现：`rollout()` 增加默认关闭的 `include_execution_trace`。打开时逐决策保留 `decision_id`、时间戳、计划/幂等 ID、Actor/Soft/Hard 目标、取整目标、数量变化、计划原因和原生敞口；用客户端订单 ID 关联原生订单终态与 fill 数量/价格/费用，并为未成交报告 `REJECTED`、`CANCELED`、`EXPIRED` 或 `unknown`。非提交动作记录提交状态及相关挂单结果。默认 report 仅输出漏斗汇总；公开 `backtest` 和 `diagnose-train` CLI 使用 `--execution-trace` 才输出明细。紧凑历史快照改为保留每个已观察原生订单的最后状态，避免订单完成后 Nautilus 缩减缓存导致 trace 无状态；同时将 `position_id` 纳入紧凑 Position 字段。

## 2026-10-17 M0.1 现金流接口边界、M0.2 默认 CI Quote 与 M2.2 决策 ID

- M0.1 接口核查：锁定环境 NautilusTrader `2.0.0rc6` 的 `AccountState` 公共型定义提供 `balances`、`margins`、`reported`、`event_id`、`info`、`ts_event` 等字段，但无标准化的 deposit/withdrawal 类型或原因字段。对 Engine 原生 ETHUSDT 8 小时回放的只读诊断观察到初始 `reported=True` 账户状态，和 08:00 Funding settlement 对齐的新 reported 状态；两者 `info={}`。因此 `reported=True` 不能当作入金/出金分类依据。尝试性判定会错误拒绝正常 Funding，已撤回，不把错误启发式留在代码中。当前权益残差可拒绝净未解释现金变动，但无法证明彼此抵消的转账与漏记项；M0.1 继续限制为无外部现金流范围，真实账户现金流分类标为 `BLOCKED`（需外部带类型/原因的转账源）。

- M2.2 最小源码修复：`NautilusBarrierEnv.step` 现在在每步 info 输出原始 `decision_id`、`plan_id`、`idempotency_key`、`delta_qty`、取消订单 ID；SUBMIT 计划按 Nautilus Adapter 相同的 SHA256 算法给出预期 `client_order_id`，NOOP 返回 `None`。历史工厂中的 NOOP 与 SUBMIT 固定样例核对决策时刻、零/非零数量、未造假订单 ID，以及 SUBMIT 的客户端 ID 与原生成交 ID 对齐。完整聚合、终态映射和未成交原因仍未实现。

## 2026-10-17 M0.1 持仓期间线性权益核对与 90 天 Train 回放

- 实现：`reconcile_linear_equity` 在已有 flat-barrier 权益复算上增加 open-barrier mark-to-market 核对。仅当原生 Position 提供 side、quantity、单位 multiplier、非 inverse 标志、结算币种，且有正的原生 mark 时才核对；持仓数量必须与逐笔成交带符号累计严格一致。公式为 `初始权益 + 净持仓数量 × mark - Σ(带符号成交数量 × 成交价) - 手续费 + 带符号 Funding`。实际 fill price 已包含执行滑点，因此滑点仅作成本归因，不再从权益扣一次。报告压缩保留 mark 与最小 Position 经济字段，不再把每步 Position 成交/调整历史复制进账本。
- 回归：手算线性开仓/mark/平仓并包含手续费、负 Funding；人为修改持仓屏障权益会失败。真实 ETHUSDT 2024-01-01 归档 golden 覆盖 480 个快照（477 个策略步），含 475 个开仓 mark 屏障、5 个 flat 屏障；475/475 个持仓屏障及终态全部完成，0 未核对，最大残差 `0`，既有终态权益 `9998.21338803`、3 个 fill、1 个 Funding 与经济指标保持不变。对应 `tests/test_real_golden_ethusdt.py`。
- 长窗回放：复用此前冻结的 Train-inner 市场、scenario rules、candidate bundle 与 scaler，执行 90 天 `long`（目标敞口 0.25）路径，不训练、不选参；登记新的开发诊断用途。129,597 个策略步、129,600 个快照，其中 129,598 个持仓屏障全部核对，0 个未核对，2 个 flat 屏障核对，最大权益残差 `0`；终态已平仓。final equity `10052.48862672`，费用 `0.28397867`、Funding `-14.65498461`、slippage attribution `0.05776`、描述性净收益 `0.5249%`。本数值只证明账本一致性，不是收益门；真实历史规则仍未得到证实。
- 证据：[m0_1_native_equity_90d.json](audit_evidence/20261017/m0_1_native_equity_90d.json)，SHA-256 `AB3ADF1C347CC3F665E47C7C29FE6558B481F941598E555F07225C6BBA68B7E7`；再现器 [reconcile_native_equity_long_20261017.py](../qa/reconcile_native_equity_long_20261017.py)，使用既有 F7 冻结计划 `.artifacts/audit-development-20261016/f7-growth-v2/plan.json`。90 天训练窗从 2024-01-15 延伸至 2024-04-14；无 Validation/Test/Holdout 访问。合成 DTO 的[线性清算账本交叉证据](audit_evidence/20261017/m0_1_synthetic_linear_liquidation.json)，SHA-256 `E6157F3CDF5D92E1EB0FCE4CB1CB9126F0CF333F093194569D315A1A5C9416A3`；[Nautilus Engine 原生线性强平证据](audit_evidence/20261017/m0_1_linear_native_liquidation.json)，SHA-256 `1D12ECA7ABC174A87B5417037754DD225946DB62C2605E07A5AF871841722A64`。

## 2026-10-17 M2.2 执行 trace、M2.1 Episode 时域与 M2.3 Bar 成本压力

- M2.2 实现：`rollout()` 增加默认关闭的 `include_execution_trace`。启用时逐决策保留 `decision_id`、时间戳、计划/幂等 ID、Actor/Soft/Hard 目标、取整目标、数量变化、计划原因和原生敞口；用客户端订单 ID 关联原生订单终态与 fill 数量/价格/费用，并为未成交报告 `REJECTED`、`CANCELED`、`EXPIRED` 或 `unknown`。非提交动作记录提交状态及相关挂单结果。默认 report 仅输出漏斗汇总；公开 `backtest` 和 `diagnose-train` CLI 使用 `--execution-trace` 才输出明细。紧凑历史快照保留已观察订单的最后状态，避免已完成订单从 Nautilus 缓存消失；Position 摘要包含 `position_id`。
- M2.2 验证：历史工厂、原生训练、应用 CLI 相关集 **94 passed**；最新默认 Windows PR 选择 **478 passed、60 deselected、38 warnings、115.26 秒**。Ruff、MyPy 对变更源码通过。trace 集成回归验证真实合成原生 Engine 的 decision→order→fills 连接、费用及订单终态；CLI 测试确认 flag 透传。完整 PR 运行在后续 M2.1 compact `position_id` 字段加入之前；该字段随后由两个定向历史工厂测试及 T0/T1 实际回放覆盖。
- M2.1 输入：复用 `.artifacts/audit-development-20261016/f7-growth-v2/plan.json` 的 Train-inner 市场/规则与候选 bundle；bundle SHA-256 `ea2ea516780f25756c7dae35636ee59eca7e3443a24259c6b58526b2536fec68`，市场 SHA-256 `06323e909997909dee35b4abf1543444ae230f30cbc1f672ded033b3bc41fe70`，规则 SHA-256 `690292b508ce722d6ba2f5b9fb745671f56fc396d2026943fd36668e9028c93b`。窗口 2024-01-15 00:00 至 2024-04-14 00:00 UTC，seed 102，bundle 执行配置/SoftRisk 参数；固定 target exposure 0.25。Base Bar 费用是假设而非历史规则认证。项目登记用途为 Train-inner development，没有 Validation/Test/Holdout 查询。
- M2.1 初始 r2 结果（已被 R3 全覆盖重跑取代）：T0 连续账户 129,597 决策，权益 10,000→10,052.48862672 USDT，PnL `+52.48862672`、费用 `0.28397867`、Funding `-14.65498461`、3 fills、1 个 Position 身份、持仓 `7,775,820` 秒，终态平仓。T1 初始版本 90 个互斥日 lane、每个 1,439 步，90 个账户均以 10,000 USDT 独立起始并终态平仓；独立 PnL 和 `+21.44852187`（不复利）、费用 `22.40042008`、Funding `-13.91367805`、180 fills、90 个 Position 身份、持仓 `7,770,600` 秒；终止平仓 90 fills、专属费用 `11.21465070`。T1−T0 的 PnL、费用、Funding、fill 差分别为 `-31.04010485`、`+22.11644141`、`+0.74130656`、`+177`。该旧结果少覆盖 87 个决策点，不用于当前结论；R3 完整覆盖结果及限制见本文件末尾的 M2.1/A03 修正记录。
- M2.1 资源与证据：最低可用物理内存 `12,778,246,144` 字节，高于停止线 `2,147,483,648` 字节。摘要 [m2_1_episode_horizon_train_inner.json](audit_evidence/20261017/m2_1_episode_horizon_train_inner.json)，SHA-256 `136922fcaa90aaecdafb0b18cf6cb2bd7f9d992048c4dac8c00e1b904c05a16b`；执行清单 [m2_1_episode_horizon_execution_manifest.json](audit_evidence/20261017/m2_1_episode_horizon_execution_manifest.json)，SHA-256 `ba921e097535c1d3362c6c0aa525ab90ca95e9dd59e483852127316f02723a8c`，绑定 10 个源码/测试文件、计划/bundle/数据哈希和项目级访问登记。正式输出 `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m2-1-t0-t1-daily-r2/`。两次前置失败分别由 runner 未继承 source plan seed 和未按窗口裁剪 Funding schedule 引起；均保留日志，最终 runner 已修复两点并完成回放。M2.1 标记 `PASS`（T0/T1 诊断；未开发 T2）。定向回归：M2.1 强制清仓对照与 M2.2 trace 测试 **2 passed、2.69 秒**；M2.1 runner Ruff/MyPy 通过。

- M2.3 结果：Base 与 Stress 都是 **129,597 决策、3/3 原生订单完整成交、0 partial/0 unfilled、数量 fill ratio 1.0、成交数量 0.202**，成交 quote notional 分别 `567.95733` 与 `567.93042` USDT。Base 净 PnL `+52.48862672`、净回报 `+0.5248863%`、最大回撤 `0.9193882%`、手续费 `0.28397867`、滑点 `0.05776`、Funding `-14.65498461`；Stress 净 PnL `+51.97778497`、净回报 `+0.5197778%`、最大回撤 `0.9194172%`、手续费 `0.56793042`、滑点 `0.28465`、Funding `-14.65498461`。Stress−Base：净 PnL `-0.51084175` USDT、费用 `+0.28395175`、滑点 `+0.22689`、Funding `0`；gross return before reported costs 两臂相同（约 `0.6748535%`）。该结果把已建模费用压力与 PnL 差对应起来；由于 Bar fill model 提供订单同量的模拟深度，100% ratio 不能认证现实可成交性，Quote/Depth 历史深度仍未验证。
- M2.3 资源/证据/检查：最低可用物理内存 `12,566,274,048` 字节，停止线 `2,147,483,648` 字节；汇总 [m2_3_bar_cost_base_stress_train_inner.json](audit_evidence/20261017/m2_3_bar_cost_base_stress_train_inner.json)，SHA-256 `a66cf6e85563f35d71dfc89c07647bc9acb080a8d73ba85d6ffe8a7806e67ed8`；执行清单 [m2_3_bar_cost_execution_manifest.json](audit_evidence/20261017/m2_3_bar_cost_execution_manifest.json)，SHA-256 `ef9b0f2d53351a817442f6b1c6b629adeedf900d135834e3988dd6e630e790a7`，绑定 10 个执行源码哈希、冻结输入和项目级 development 登记。产物 `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m2-3-bar-cost-v1/`。runner Ruff、MyPy、带工作区 `PYTHONPATH` 的 `--help` 和 90 天双臂回放均通过；首次未设置 `qa` 导入路径时 `--help` 启动失败，已用实际复现命令中的 `PYTHONPATH=packages/*/src;qa` 修正环境。

## 2026-10-17 M2.4 Episode Reset 剖析与只读缓存

- **热点证据与窗口准入：** 原始工厂在同一 60 天 episode 两次 Reset 均值 3.62 秒，360 天均值 20.01 秒；每次都重复遍历可用事件、转换 Nautilus MarketEvent、按可见时间分组、对每个 decision 重新切分可见 batch 并重建全部 BarrierFrame。项目研究登记的只读元数据显示 2024-01-15 起的 360 天会穿过 2024-07-16 至 07-31 的已预留 Holdout，准入请求被拒绝且未读取行情；后续冻结安全窗口从 2024-08-01 起，60/360 天都避开正式 Test/Holdout。数据用途仅 `Train-inner development`，无正式评价查询。
- **实现：** `HistoricalEnvironmentFactory` 懒建并复用按 availability timestamp 排序的原生市场 batch 索引、逐决策 batch 增量组和不可变 BarrierFrame。episode 起点为 0 时直接复用帧；后续轮换起点只按该 Episode 的 warmup 窗口重建第一帧，其余帧引用缓存。原生账户、Engine、快照仍按 Episode 新建；Market batches 仍遵守原有决策可见性和 warmup 窗口。
- **回归：** 新增事件索引对原始 `available_event_window` 分组、episode offset batch partition、缓存 frame 与原批次分组相同的测试；新增明确重建 uncached frames 的参考路径，先执行前缀、导出 Replay，再由缓存工厂恢复并继续，核对 Replay 边界观测/token、逐步 observation/reward/done/info、费用后 metrics、成交经济字段、最终原生权益与持仓。`tests/test_historical_factory.py` 与 `tests/test_native_training_episode.py` 最终 **72 passed，28 第三方 warnings，86.46 秒**；Ruff 通过，Windows MyPy 对历史工厂和剖析脚本 **2 source files** 通过。
- **60 天基线→缓存后：** 两次无缓存 Reset 均值 `3.6175s`；缓存冷/热 Reset `3.1255s / 0.0171s`，含缓存构造的均值 `1.5713s`；缓存 86,443 visible batches、86,400 rows。完整 rollout `33.7665s → 29.2720s`（快 15.35%），86,397 决策，订单/fill/Funding event 数 `2/2/179`，终态平仓，net return 完全相同 `-0.004541307482016754`。Reset 峰值 RSS 增量 `259,268,608 → 241,299,456` bytes。
- **360 天基线→缓存后：** 两次无缓存 Reset 均值 `20.0073s`；缓存冷/热 Reset `19.9961s / 0.0704s`，含缓存构造的均值 `10.0333s`；缓存 518,708 visible batches、518,400 decision rows。完整 rollout `228.8276s → 204.0579s`（快 12.14%），518,397 决策，订单/fill/Funding event 数 `5/5/1,079`，终态平仓，net return 完全相同 `0.008085841677032857`。Reset 峰值 RSS 增量 `1,201,213,440 → 1,221,619,712` bytes；缓存保留的内存与原重建峰值同量级，最小可用物理内存 `10,848,882,688` bytes，停止线 `2,147,483,648` bytes。冷构建接近原 Reset 用时，收益来自同工厂多次 Episode 复用；不承诺一次性 Reset 同等提速。收益值只作 scenario-only 工程经济等价校验，不是策略收益证据。
- **证据和命令：** 基线摘要 `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m2-4-reset-profile-v3/summary.json` SHA-256 `c673bfec9c2d982facddc36c2c03b41dae3d8ccdcdfc7e2a671ddcc95c94e978`；缓存后计划、60/360 分项报告和摘要位于 `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m2-4-reset-profile-v6/`，摘要 SHA-256 `c0f531f15df1f8e37d92d0f23d2af01729766f5602abda84f2e073a3155028bd`。冻结输入 `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m2-3-bar-cost-v1/plan.json`，数据/rules、bundle 与源码哈希写在两份 profile plan/summary 中。实际执行：

```powershell
$workspacePythonPath = ((Get-ChildItem packages -Directory | ForEach-Object { Join-Path $_.FullName 'src' }) -join ';')
$env:PYTHONPATH = "$workspacePythonPath;qa"
$env:MYPYPATH = $workspacePythonPath
.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q tests\test_historical_factory.py tests\test_native_training_episode.py -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp .artifacts\audit-development-20261017\pytest-m2-4-verified --tb=short
.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m ruff check packages\gym_bridge\src\rles_gym_bridge\historical.py tests\test_historical_factory.py qa\profile_episode_reset_20261017.py
.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m mypy --platform win32 packages\gym_bridge\src\rles_gym_bridge\historical.py qa\profile_episode_reset_20261017.py
.venv\Scripts\python.exe qa\profile_episode_reset_20261017.py --plan .artifacts\audit-development-20261017\m2-3-bar-cost-v1\plan.json --output .artifacts\audit-development-20261017\m2-4-reset-profile-v6
```

- **状态/续做：** M2.4 标记 `PASS`（重复 Episode Reset 性能与工程等价）。60/360 天两次相同 seed 的初始 observation/native equity 指纹一致；离线 uncached 参考 frame 与缓存恢复 Replay 逐步一致。活动游标推进到 M3.1（A05）：盘点候选/seed/特征/人工挑选/失败尝试历史，评估现有 DSR 试验数来源；不能可靠估计的历史覆盖标为 `not_estimated`。

## 2026-10-17 M3.1 DSR 试验数来源与覆盖界限

- `ResearchLedger.evidence_summary()` 现在额外输出 `trial_count_inventory`，列出当前协议已观察候选 ID、fold ID、各类原始查询、ES lane 请求数和阶段回执数；明确这些读数不估计项目历史独立试验数，并列出未记录的失败/未查询尝试、非规范化参数等价、跨协议 seed/feature、人工挑选和未登记研究。
- 共用 `ProjectResearchRegistry` 新增 `trial_attempt` 与追加式 `trial_attempt_event` 表及禁止 UPDATE/DELETE 的触发器。WFO 对每个固定候选登记 recipe 内容/哈希、特征哈希与有效 seed；启动、选择/未选择、Python 异常失败和中断都留下幂等事件。相同协议恢复不覆盖身份或既有事件；当前 WFO DSR 证据携带该协议尝试清单与项目登记总数。
- WFO 的 `deflated_sharpe` 结果现在附带 `trial_count_evidence`：冻结当前候选 ID 与哈希、账本观察覆盖、候选 ID 去重边界、跨折处理、DSR 独立候选 Sharpe 假设、项目登记尝试以及项目历史 `not_estimated` 原因；明确不得解读为全历史选择校正显著性。新增 `current_protocol_sensitivity`，固定观测到的候选 Sharpe 离散度，比较 effective trials=1（完全依赖端点）与当前候选数（独立端点）；该范围是模型场景敏感性，不是从数据估计的相关系数，历史敏感性仍 `not_estimated`。
- 验证：`tests/test_research_access.py tests/test_research_ledger.py tests/test_research_wfo.py tests/test_orchestrator_workflow.py tests/test_source_manifest.py tests/test_research_metrics.py` **108 passed**；相关源码、QA 盘点脚本与测试 Ruff 通过，5 个源码/QA 文件 MyPy 通过，`git diff --check` 通过。使用工作区 `.venv` 与显式 MYPYPATH/PYTHONPATH；`uv run` 因沙箱禁止写入用户级 uv interpreter cache 失败，未影响工作区环境测试。
- 旧访问清单只读复核：10 个 WFO 协议库（8 ABORTED、1 CLOSED、1 SUSPENDED）、21 条 Validation 与 1 条 Test 查询，18 个协议/候选组合和 10 个候选 ID；另有 22 条 standalone 报告索引、67 条访问登记、0 个未解决的访问边界。清单限于两个已知旧研究根，不能证明同名候选 ID 的参数等价、seed/feature 去重，也不覆盖人工筛选和未入账失败研究。
- 新增可复现离线盘点 qa/research_trial_inventory.py：验证冻结 v2 清单和每个旧 SQLite SHA，在只读连接中读取 protocol/query 元数据，并只扫描两个指定根内的 config JSON。11 个匹配配置文件显示 10 种候选参数/seed/feature 组合指纹（不是有效独立试验估计）；artifact docs/audit_evidence/20261017/legacy_research_trial_inventory.json 复跑逐字段相同，未加载行情或正式 Test/Holdout 数据。SHA-256 3c5b434ed055d2ad2482d82ea6e5d938452d3c2cc2d9df3a3a9927fb02a743ee；盘点脚本 Ruff 与 MyPy 通过。
- **状态/续做：** M3.1 标记 `PARTIAL` 并停止依赖缺失历史的扩展：当前协议 DSR 有 effective trials=1 到候选数的场景端点敏感性，但没有同一时间切片的历史候选回报来估算相关结构，也无法从两个已知旧根还原人工筛选和未登记失败实验。项目历史有效试验数继续 `not_estimated`。独立推进到 M3.2：核查已有 Train-inner 回放的交互步数、唯一市场时点、Episode 重叠、持仓与市场 Regime 覆盖；不得将重复采样当成独立样本。

## 2026-10-17 M3.2 Train-inner 覆盖与训练诊断

- 训练环境每步诊断现包含冻结 Episode 窗口、当前可见 L2 市场特征和 native net exposure。`TrainingDiagnostics` 对每次 `learn()` 汇总唯一决策时间戳/重复采样率、Episode 窗口重叠对、flat/long/short 样本数，以及每项市场特征的计数、均值、标准差、最小值和最大值；另外以本 `learn()` Train 样本的波动四分位和趋势/Funding 正负零分组，报告边界、样本数和逐箱 `r_econ` 求和/均值。此为训练块内描述统计，阈值随块变化，不用于在线决策，也不能作为跨块统一尺度或独立验证。
- `R1-learning.json` 写盘 schema 升至 `rles.r1-learning.v3`；恢复验证继续接受 v1/v2，且 bundle 仍对学习诊断整体做哈希绑定。
- 新增 `qa/analyze_stage58_train_coverage.py` 和证据 `docs/audit_evidence/20261017/stage58_train_coverage_analysis.json`，只读取既有 preregistration/config/learning 记录并校验其 SHA，不加载行情。Stage58 四窗共 32,768 交互步、32,768 唯一决策时间点、窗内/窗间重复为 0；每窗一个 8,192 步 Episode，四个决策区间无重叠；Validation/Test/Holdout/outer-2026 查询均为 0。四窗日期已有 Train 研究复用，故本次唯一覆盖不构成新鲜独立市场证据。
- Stage58 的 v2 记录没有持仓方向或逐步市场状态，不能回溯计算状态覆盖/分组经济贡献；Stage59 已在四个已复用 Train 窗口生成 v3 字段和逐箱经济贡献；M3.2 保持 `PARTIAL`，剩余条件是采用新增且登记的 Train-inner 数据、冻结可跨窗比较的 Regime 边界，并在获准时检验其对 Validation 的解释/改善能力。
- 验证：`tests/test_native_training_episode.py tests/test_historical_factory.py tests/test_source_manifest.py` **74 passed**；修改源码/测试 Ruff 通过；`env.py`、`provider.py`、`rl_stage.py` 与覆盖分析脚本 MyPy **4 files** 通过；相关源码、测试和 QA Ruff 通过，`git diff --check` 通过。首次测试使用过期安装包失败，切换显式工作区 `PYTHONPATH` 后按源码执行并通过。
- **状态/续做：** M3.2 标记 `PARTIAL`；活动游标留在 M3.2，等下一次有登记的 Train-inner learner 产生 v3 状态分布和动作/Regime 贡献。现阶段不读取额外市场数据，不把重复计算步计为独立样本。

- **冻结与输入：** 新增 `qa/preregister_stage60_fixed_regimes.py`，对 Stage58/Stage59 已冻结配置和来源做哈希核验，冻结四个既用 Train 窗口（2024-01-01、2024-09-01、2025-05-01、2026-01-01）、PPO seed 102、每窗 8,192 步/一个 Episode、源码 SHA、Regime 边界和零评价查询。登记文件 `docs/audit_evidence/20261017/stage60_fixed_regimes/preregistration.json` SHA-256 `afc8c1d5f1c87791c56e487a7fc77ab9e9b9b4e8ab6733cbf950a2c5c3fd2220`；四个冻结 config 与来源身份在同一目录。目的仅为已用 Train 日期的诊断复现，不进行模型选择，不产生独立样本声明。

## 2026-10-17 M0.1 explicit external cashflow facts in native ledger

- **Implementation:** `iter_account_rows`, `account_rows`, `native_report` and `reconcile_linear_equity` accept caller-supplied `external_cashflows`. Each fact requires `event_id`, `decision_ts_ns`, account `currency`, `kind` (`DEPOSIT` or `WITHDRAWAL`), positive finite `amount`, nonempty `source_id`, and a lowercase 64-character `source_sha256`. Signed deposits/withdrawals are included in sampled returns and the cumulative native equity equation; reports retain the unique source-identified facts and per-barrier cumulative flow. Flows must align with a unique sampling barrier after the event-free initial barrier. Identical duplicate IDs are idempotent; conflicting IDs, unsupported types, bad amounts/currencies, missing/invalid source identity, off-grid times and duplicate barrier times fail closed. Existing callers with no explicit flows keep their prior zero-flow behavior. No inference uses `AccountState.reported`, balances, or unexplained equity changes.
- **Scope limit:** The SHA field identifies the caller's source artifact but this API does not open or authenticate that artifact, and cannot establish that a transfer source is complete or truthful. Nautilus `AccountState` exposes no standardized transfer kind/reason, so no native adapter currently supplies these facts. Production external cashflow completeness remains `BLOCKED` pending a trusted, typed transfer source. Reconciliation supports the explicit supplied facts; it does not infer hidden or mutually offsetting movements.
- **Regression and observed failure:** Added synthetic deposit/withdrawal reconciliation on flat barriers, a deposit while a marked linear position is open, exact duplicate idempotency, conflicting event ID rejection, invalid SHA rejection, and ambiguous duplicate-timestamp rejection. An initial test expected nominal PnL divided by initial balance; it failed because `AccountFactsAccumulator` reports time-weighted returns across the deposit boundary. The test now asserts the explicit chained time-weighted formula. Final metrics suite: `tests/test_research_metrics.py` **31 passed**. On the unchanged implementation plus the pre-existing tests, `tests/test_research_metrics.py tests/test_historical_factory.py tests/test_real_golden_ethusdt.py` **78 passed, 18 third-party warnings, 64.60s**; the added open-position cashflow case separately passes in the final 31-test metrics suite. Ruff passed for `native_metrics.py` and its tests; Windows MyPy passed for `native_metrics.py`.
- **Command examples (repository PowerShell):**

  ```powershell
  $workspacePythonPath = ((Get-ChildItem packages -Directory | ForEach-Object { Join-Path $_.FullName 'src' }) -join ';')
  $env:PYTHONPATH = "$workspacePythonPath;qa"
  .venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q --basetemp=.artifacts\audit-development-20261017\pytest-m01-explicit-cashflow-focused-final tests\test_research_metrics.py
  .venv\Scripts\ruff.exe check packages\research\src\rles_research\native_metrics.py tests\test_research_metrics.py
  $env:MYPYPATH = $workspacePythonPath
  .venv\Scripts\mypy.exe --platform win32 packages\research\src\rles_research\native_metrics.py
  ```
- **Status and next work:** M0.1 remains `PARTIAL`; the audit ledger can now reconcile independently identified transfer facts, but actual source integration/authorization and real account transfer evidence remain absent. See the subsequent Binance export adapter entry. No formal Validation/Test/Holdout data was queried.

## 2026-10-17 M0.1 Binance USDⓈ-M transfer export adapter

- **Official source contract:** Binance USDⓈ-M [`Get Income History`](https://developers.binance.com/docs/derivatives/usds-margined-futures/account/rest-api/Get-Income-History) documents `/fapi/v1/income`, `incomeType=TRANSFER`, `income`, `asset`, `time` in milliseconds and `tranId`; `tranId` is unique only for a user within the same income type, and history is limited to the latest three months. The endpoint can return all flow types when `incomeType` is omitted, so the importer requires every supplied record to be `TRANSFER` and fails closed on mixed input.
- **Implementation:** Added `rles_research.binance_cashflows.import_binance_usdm_transfer_income`, accepting exact archived JSON bytes/text, account scope, expected settlement currency and strictly increasing sample barriers. It hashes exact payload bytes, scopes stable event IDs by account and `TRANSFER`/`tranId`, maps positive amounts to deposits and negative amounts to withdrawals, and maps each millisecond event to the first sample barrier at or after it. It rejects malformed JSON/schema, unsupported/mixed income types, wrong currency, zero/nonfinite amounts, bad IDs/times, conflicting duplicate IDs, and transfers outside post-initial barriers. Returned limitations explicitly require the caller to verify signed query scope, account identity, pagination/window coverage and source origin. It cannot recover events outside Binance's documented retention period.
- **Integration and validation:** Added integration coverage that imports a deposit and withdrawal and passes them into `native_report`, reconciling the resulting `+100.25 USDT` flow with zero linear equity residual. Additional tests cover invalid/mixed payloads, currency mismatch, zero amount, pre-initial event time, conflicting transaction IDs and duplicate barriers. `tests/test_binance_cashflows.py tests/test_research_metrics.py`: **39 passed**. Ruff on importer/export/tests passed; Windows MyPy on importer passed.
- **Status:** M0.1 remains `PARTIAL`. The adapter makes exported, typed transfer rows usable in the ledger; it does not implement signed live collection, prove pagination coverage, validate Binance account identity, or independently compare against a complete account statement. No account credentials were used and no formal Validation/Test/Holdout data was queried. Continue with another unblocked development package after recording this boundary.

## 2026-10-17 M0.3 same-owner formal-read relabel guard

- **Gap found:** The project reservation query allowed a same-owner `test` interval to be reserved again as `development` under a different key. It also permitted same-owner overlapping Test keys beyond the fixed WFO query/component pairing. This could make a consumed formal window look like ordinary development use in a new output/protocol record.
- **Change:** Overlapping formal Test/Holdout records now fail closed across purpose changes and query IDs. The only shared Test exception requires identical owner, exact interval, protocol/query ID, source/bundle/recipe/code identities, and the distinct `policy`/`baselines` components. Holdout has no second-key sharing. The existing WFO order—declare the same owner's pre-Holdout development range first, then reserve specific frozen fold Test queries—remains supported. Historical entries remain imported atomically as observations and do not authorize reads.
- **Observed implementation failures and correction:** The first patch accidentally dedented registry methods behind the helper and caused 13 failures/MyPy errors; restoring class structure exposed two legitimate WFO development→Test overlap resumptions, which were then added as a narrowly scoped ordering exception. A subsequent M1.2 test initially forgot to mark its v2 EchoLane insolvent; the fixture was corrected, not the implementation.
- **Validation:** `tests/test_research_access.py tests/test_application_cli.py tests/test_research_wfo.py` passed **94 tests** in 3.56 seconds with explicit workspace `PYTHONPATH`; this includes the interrupted/resumed fold Test path. Ruff on access registry and its tests passed; Windows MyPy on `access.py` passed. M0.3 remains `PARTIAL` because pre-registry historical access coverage cannot be reconstructed from existing evidence.

## 2026-10-17 M1.2 candidate-level feasibility-first aggregation v2

- **Gap found:** v1 assigns -2 to each infeasible episode and bounds feasible episode scores to (-1,1), but `dispatch_generation` averaged all episode values. One infeasible episode could therefore be diluted by many high-scoring episodes; a candidate with any hard-constraint failure could outscore a fully feasible candidate. This contradicted the stated constraint-first ranking.
- **Change:** Preserved `constraint_priority_train_inner_v1` and its evaluator/checkpoint identity. Added `constraint_priority_train_inner_v2`; its candidate selection fitness is -2 whenever any episode is infeasible, otherwise it is the mean bounded economic score. The raw mean and infeasible count remain in evidence, with `selection_fitness` added to candidate constraint evidence. V2 adds an evaluator identity bit and a distinct dispatcher protocol signature, preventing checkpoint/replay from silently crossing semantics. Application configuration accepts both explicit versions; legacy weighted scoring remains unchanged/default.
- **Regression:** Added a four-scenario case where candidate A has one native liquidation and three high scores (raw mean +0.175) while candidate B is fully feasible at -0.5. The v2 optimizer input ranks A at -2 and B at -0.5; the raw mean remains visible in evidence. Added a v1/v2 dispatcher-signature distinction and v2 config parsing test.
- **Validation:** `tests/test_es_fitness.py tests/test_compute_worker_pool.py tests/test_es_checkpoint_resume.py tests/test_application_cli.py` passed **68 tests**, 12 third-party warnings, 9.96 seconds. Ruff on touched ES/orchestrator sources and tests passed; Windows MyPy on five touched source files passed. No new Validation/Test/Holdout run was made; prior v1 Train-inner evidence is unchanged and cannot validate v2 or imply profitability.
- **Status and next work:** M1.2 engineering gate now has a versioned feasibility-first aggregate; formal Validation remains unauthorized/unavailable, so M1.2 remains `PARTIAL`. Current cursor moves to M1.3: inspect only existing frozen stage artifact identities; if required matching E1/R1/A0/A1/A3/A4 artifacts remain absent, skip that evidence-blocked comparison and continue with independent engineering work.

## 2026-10-17 M2.3 synthetic Bar depth pressure

- **Gap found:** Existing Bar Base/Stress replay varied adverse price/fees/latency but always supplied simulated book quantity equal to the full order. That can measure cost shocks, but it cannot test fill-ratio sensitivity or partial execution.
- **Change:** Added `FillProfile.simulated_depth_fraction`, defaulting to 1 to preserve the old sufficient-depth Bar contract. A stress fraction scales generated native order-book size down to the instrument quantity step. The value is frozen in execution configuration identity and scenario output/hash when non-default. Base must remain at 1; Quote requires 1 and continues using native displayed depth without a synthetic cap.
- **Native behavior regression:** A Bar stress order requested 0.500 ETH with 0.4 simulated depth; Nautilus filled 0.200 ETH (fill ratio 0.4) and retained the unfilled remainder. This validates deterministic synthetic scenario behavior only; it is not historical liquidity evidence and does not change the Quote/Depth certification boundary.
- **Validation:** With explicit workspace `PYTHONPATH`, `tests/test_execution_contract_bridge.py tests/test_nautilus_adapter_smoke.py tests/test_application_cli.py` passed **88 tests** in 2.96 seconds. Targeted Ruff passed. Windows MyPy with workspace `MYPYPATH` reported no issues in the four changed source files. `uv build --all-packages --out-dir .artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m23-depth-wheels` succeeded after placing `UV_CACHE_DIR` under the workspace; it produced 11 wheels and 11 sdists. All 11 wheels were installed with `--no-deps` into the isolated `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m23-wheel-install` target, then the same 88 tests passed against that target on `PYTHONPATH` in 3.39 seconds. The initial default uv cache attempt repeatedly hit sandbox `Access denied` and produced no artifacts; the stalled build was stopped and not counted as a pass. An initial focused run also passed 8 selected tests, including the new native partial-fill and existing Quote tests.
- **Data access:** None; no market data or research windows read.
- **Status and next work:** M2.3 Bar synthetic fill-pressure engineering is complete; Quote/Depth comparison remains `CONDITIONAL` for lack of same-window approved historical evidence. Cursor moves to M3.1/A05 to recheck the known historical trial-coverage boundary and keep unsupported project-wide effective-trial claims explicitly `not_estimated`.

## 2026-10-17 N04 whole-application Windows Job supervisor

- **Gap found:** Existing `CPUWorkerPool` Job limits covered worker trees but excluded the parent coordinator/learner process. The N04 resource-recovery objective therefore still lacked a way to cap the complete training application tree.
- **Change:** Added `WindowsWorkerJob.run_suspended()` using `CreateProcessW(CREATE_SUSPENDED)`, assigning the process to a Job before resuming its primary thread. Added `rles_compute_runtime.supervisor` with a `python -m` CLI for an arbitrary application command, a process-tree commit-memory cap, timeout handling, cleanup of surviving descendants, and an optional exclusive-create evidence JSON that records command SHA-256, exit status, memory peak and Job cleanup without storing command arguments.
- **Deterministic OS regression:** Real Windows tests cover a child writing a checkpoint then exceeding a 128 MiB Job limit, a child that exits while its descendant remains alive, and supervisor timeout cleanup. The checkpoint survives; Job cleanup reports zero active processes. The tests do not read market data or alter research query reservations.
- **Application smoke:** Ran the actual orchestrator module `--help` inside a 2 GiB Job. It exited 0, reported two processes total, peak tree commit `983,347,200` bytes and zero active processes after cleanup. Evidence: `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/native-supervisor-help.json`.
- **Validation:** Workspace tests `tests/test_windows_supervisor.py tests/test_windows_worker_limits.py tests/test_resource_recovery.py` passed **20 tests** in 9.63 seconds; Ruff passed; Windows MyPy passed for both changed compute-runtime source files. Built `rles-compute-runtime` 0.4.1 wheel/sdist, installed it with the existing contracts wheel into an isolated target, then `tests/test_windows_supervisor.py tests/test_resource_recovery.py` passed **13 tests** in 3.65 seconds against that target.
- **Status and next work:** The supervisor primitive and package path are validated; N04 remains `PARTIAL` pending higher-level real training-command capacity/recovery acceptance and independent-machine/platform confirmation. ## 2026-10-17 M1.2/A02 strict feasible-score bound and lane validation

- **Gap confirmed:** v2 feasibility-first selection assigns `-2` to a candidate with any infeasible episode, assuming feasible economic fitness lies strictly inside `(-1, 1)`. `math.tanh()` can round extreme finite inputs to exactly `-1.0` or `1.0`; the public candidate lane also accepted any finite worker fitness, so a malformed but marked-feasible result below `-2` could outrank the infeasible sentinel.
- **Change:** The v2 fitness protocol clamps floating-point saturation to the nearest representable values strictly inside `(-1, 1)`; v1 keeps the original `math.tanh()` result for checkpoint and objective compatibility. `dispatch_generation(feasibility_first=True)` now rejects a feasible result outside that strict interval before candidate aggregation. Added deterministic coverage for extreme positive/negative returns and worker results at the upper endpoint or below the infeasible sentinel.
- **Validation:** Workspace-source `tests/test_es_fitness.py tests/test_compute_worker_pool.py` passed **42 tests, 8 third-party warnings** in 9.97 seconds. After adding an explicit assertion that v1 still returns its previous floating-point endpoint, `tests/test_es_fitness.py` passed again (**12 tests**). Ruff passed on both source files and both tests; Windows MyPy passed on both source files; `git diff --check` had no whitespace errors (only existing CRLF conversion notices). Evidence output: `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m12-worker-pool-full/` and `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/m12-fitness-legacy-compat/`.
- **Data access:** None; tests use deterministic synthetic candidate facts only.
- **Status and next work:** The v2 score/sentinel ordering contract is now enforced at both fitness production and lane aggregation. ## 2026-10-17 Stage69/A07 feature preparation indexing and direct aggregation

- **Profile:** On synthetic-only data, constructed 4,232 events (4,096 one-minute bars plus periodic rules) and timed 1,000 causal feature rows using 5m/15m/60m features. The pre-change cProfile run attributed about 0.20 seconds of 1.81 seconds to repeated sorting in `_bars_1m`; 3,000 `aggregate_closed` calls remained the dominant path. No market files or formal evaluation windows were read.
- **Change:** `visible_event_window` already returns bars ordered by `(event time, availability, event ID)`. `_bars_1m` now filters that bounded view without sorting it again; the unbounded availability-ordered path retains its timestamp sort. Added regression coverage for out-of-input-order bars, delayed availability and duplicate close timestamps.
- **Rule lookup:** `CuratedStore` now builds a per-instrument prefix-maximum index ordered by `max(available_at_ns, effective_ns)`. This preserves the prior visible/filter/sort behavior while allowing a binary search per decision; expiry and same-effective-time payload conflict checks remain explicit. A deterministic reference comparison covered delayed availability, future-effective rules, expired rules and same-effective conflicts. On 136 synthetic rules and 1,000 queries, outputs matched exactly and indexed lookup was about **85x** faster than the previous scan/filter/sort path.
- **Multi-timeframe aggregation:** Bounded lookbacks now aggregate validated immutable `Event` payloads directly, avoiding an intermediate dict per 1m bar. The three-round comparison against the previous dict-based aggregation measured medians of **0.6771s legacy** and **0.4949s direct** for 1,000 multi-timeframe rows (about **26.9%** faster); all `FeatureRow` hashes matched (`86ea9e11e7fd593ed358a6bc10373da02dff0e115f526092321ded6a3dfcc826`). Added an explicit 5m aggregation golden for closed buckets and delayed availability.
- **Streaming bucket grouping:** Replaced the bounded path's per-row bucket dictionary, duplicate timestamp set and bucket sort with event-time `groupby`; duplicate timestamps are still rejected while traversing the full visible window. In a further alternating three-round 1,000-row comparison, direct dict aggregation had a **0.4910s** median and streaming grouping **0.4606s** (about **6.2%** faster); all feature-row hashes matched exactly.
- **60-day synthetic scale check:** Built 89,280 generated events (86,400 bars plus 2,880 rules) and compared 20,000 consecutive multi-timeframe decision rows. Legacy dict aggregation took **18.78s**, streaming aggregation **11.94s** (1.57x); all 20,000 row hashes matched. Store build took **1.01s**; current process working-set delta after rows was **89.2 MiB**, peak delta **114.0 MiB**. On the same 20,000 decision times, the prior rule scan took **9.188s**, indexed queries **0.006s**, with exact rule IDs. These are synthetic workload measurements, not real-market or 360-day certification.
- **360-day lazy bucket index:** Added a `CuratedStore`-scoped, lazy cache of complete multi-timeframe OHLCV buckets. Lookback queries use binary search and decision-time availability filtering; histories containing duplicate close timestamps keep the original decision-specific path. Returned rows are copied so caller mutation cannot alter the cached index. On 535,680 synthetic events and 20,000 decision times sampled across 360 days, legacy aggregation took **19.13s** and indexed feature generation including cold index construction took **2.91s** (**6.58x**); every `FeatureRow` hash matched. Store working-set delta was **484.0 MiB**, ending at **560.6 MiB**, peak delta **630.5 MiB**; minimum sampled free physical memory was **17,279 MiB**.
- **Validation:** `tests/test_data_event_guards.py tests/test_l2_gym_bridge.py` plus causal MTF and gap cases passed **29 tests**. Ruff and Windows MyPy passed on both changed source files. An alternating three-round baseline comparison against the original feature sort implementation measured medians of **0.722s legacy** and **0.693s optimized** for 1,000 rows (about **4.0%** faster); every row hash matched (`6ad808e52bc910957360a6c9c3c36f55fcd665ead28fe377da723c1f6`). A broader historical-factory run was blocked by sandbox `PermissionError` on `os.link` while writing test Parquet, not by the feature path; the relevant in-memory MTF tests passed separately.
- **Data access:** Synthetic generated events only; no Train, Validation, Test or Holdout market data was opened.
- **Status and next work:** Both 60- and 360-day synthetic workloads are now measured; neither substitutes for an authorized representative Train dataset. ## 2026-10-17 N04 PyTorch CUDA allocator budget configuration

- **Gap confirmed:** The application could request a CUDA learner and serialize its shared-user GPU lease, but had no way to budget its PyTorch caching allocator. The official [PyTorch API contract](https://docs.pytorch.org/docs/stable/generated/torch.cuda.memory.set_per_process_memory_fraction.html) says the fraction limits allocations through that caching allocator and may produce allocator OOM; it does not establish a whole-process/device VRAM ceiling.
- **Change:** Added optional `rl_gpu_memory_fraction` to application config, validating a finite numeric fraction in `(0,1]` and rejecting it for CPU runs. `RLConfig.cuda_allocator_fraction` validates the same contract, participates in learner/checkpoint identity when set, and preserves the old identity exactly when omitted. The provider applies `torch.cuda.set_per_process_memory_fraction` before constructing the training VecEnv/model and before loading a CUDA checkpoint. Bundle `runtime_resources` records the selected fraction; the frozen config is already part of `run_recipe_hash`, so resume with a changed budget fails the existing recipe check.
- **Validation:** With explicit workspace source paths (`PYTHONPATH=packages/*/src`) and the workspace Python 3.14 venv, `tests/test_compute_device.py tests/test_application_cli.py tests/test_l4_provider_smoke.py` plus the synthetic application bundle/backtest and partial-R1 resume integration cases passed **75 tests** (8 existing third-party warnings). Coverage includes config parsing, invalid/CPU-only budgets, learner identity changes, exact default identity compatibility, the CUDA allocator API call/device selection and unchanged CPU bundle metadata. A separate CUDA-marked provider integration passed: PyTorch read back fraction `0.9`, and a synthetic two-step PPO learner created and trained under that budget (**1 passed**, one expected SB3 MLP-on-GPU efficiency warning). Ruff passed on all touched source/test files; Windows MyPy passed on the three changed source modules. No market data was used. The default `uv` interpreter cache denied writes, so tests used pre-existing venvs with explicit package-source paths; an initial stale installed-package run was discarded and is not counted.
- **Boundary:** PyTorch documents this as a caching-allocator limit. CUDA context, cuDNN/cuBLAS or other native allocations and other processes may consume additional VRAM. The CUDA smoke proves the configured provider call and a small synthetic learner update, not a whole-process VRAM cap, production workload envelope, cross-OS-user coordination, or hosted-platform validation; N04 remains `PARTIAL`.
- **Next cursor:** The configurable allocator budget and learner identity are now implemented. Inspect the remaining N04 gates and then continue the first independent audit item that can be implemented without new data authorization or hosted runner access.

## 2026-10-17 N04 kernel-owned CUDA lease crash recovery

- **Gap confirmed:** The shared-user GPU lease used `O_EXCL` metadata as both owner record and lock. A hard exit left the file forever, even after Windows released the owner's CUDA context; deleting it based only on stale-looking PID metadata could supersede an unknown live process.
- **Change:** `GPULearnerLease` now holds a kernel-managed exclusive byte lock for the lifetime of the acquiring process (`msvcrt.locking` on Windows, `flock` on POSIX) and keeps the stable lock-file inode instead of unlinking it. Metadata protocol `rles.gpu-lease.v2` is rewritten only while the kernel lock is held. A hard exit releases the OS lock, allowing a new owner to take over. Existing unmarked/legacy or corrupt owner files remain fail-closed and unchanged for manual recovery. A same-process path registry preserves contention semantics on Windows, where the byte lock belongs to a process.
- **Regression:** Added an actual spawned-process hard-kill test: a contender is rejected while the owner lives, then successfully acquires and releases after the owner process is killed. Added legacy-file fail-closed coverage and adjusted normal release to verify the stable file is empty rather than absent.
- **Validation:** Workspace-source Windows run of `tests/test_compute_device.py tests/test_compute_worker_pool.py tests/test_windows_worker_limits.py tests/test_resource_recovery.py tests/test_windows_supervisor.py -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp .tmp/pytest-compute-runtime-20261017`: **66 passed, 8 warnings**. Ruff passed for `resources.py` and `test_compute_device.py`; MyPy passed for `resources.py` under both `--platform win32` and `--platform linux`; `git diff --check` reported no whitespace errors. `uv build --package rles-compute-runtime` produced wheel and sdist; installing the wheel into `.artifacts/audit-development-20261017/n04-gpu-kernel-lease-install/` confirmed imports resolve from that wheel and report protocol v2. No market or account data was accessed.
- **Boundary at this checkpoint:** This first step recovered the shared OS-level GPU lock for another output, but left the old run's SQLite CPU/RAM quota row intact. The next entry below adds exact recovery for runs launched by the Windows Job supervisor; unsupervised runs remain manual-recovery-only.

## 2026-10-17 N04 supervised application GPU quota recovery

- **Gap confirmed:** The application stores its quota in `<run output>/quota.sqlite` and acquires that SQLite lease before attempting the shared GPU lock. The kernel GPU lock alone therefore could not resume the same output after a hard exit. The existing Windows supervisor already owns the complete application process tree and can provide a stronger recovery boundary when that process exits.
- **Change:** The Windows supervisor now assigns each application a unique named `Local\\RLES-Supervisor-*` Job and passes its name and exact commit-memory ceiling through inherited environment variables. CUDA `ComputeAllocation` enrolls its exact lease only when it can prove the current coordinator belongs to that Job, then seals the enrollment before learner construction. Added `recover-supervised-gpu`: it requires the exact lease descriptor, a sealed enrollment, exact original owner exit/PID lifetime proof, an empty or absent matching Job, and a successful acquire/release probe of the shared kernel GPU lock before atomically deleting the SQLite lease and writing a receipt. The read-only inspect command now distinguishes `application_preparing` and `application_ready` GPU leases. Legacy/unsupervised GPU rows remain unsupported by this recovery command.
- **Regression:** A Windows end-to-end test launches a real supervised Python process, acquires a GPU lease without requiring CUDA hardware, verifies recovery is refused while that owner is alive, hard-kills it, checks supervisor Job cleanup, invokes the recovery CLI against the same quota database, validates the receipt, and reacquires/releases the same GPU lease and quota. Also preserves normal cross-process exclusion and legacy owner-file behavior.
- **Validation:** `tests/test_compute_device.py tests/test_compute_worker_pool.py tests/test_windows_worker_limits.py tests/test_resource_recovery.py tests/test_windows_supervisor.py -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp .tmp/pytest-compute-runtime-final2-20261017`: **67 passed, 8 warnings**. Ruff passed on all six changed runtime modules and two tests. MyPy passed on those six modules under both `--platform win32` and `--platform linux`; full `mypy --platform win32 packages qa` passed **138 source files**. `uv build --package rles-compute-runtime` produced wheel and sdist; the installed wheel CLI lists `recover-supervised-gpu`. The full Windows default PR selector also passed: **533 passed, 1 skipped, 67 deselected, 38 warnings**. No market/account data was accessed.
- **Boundary / next cursor:** Same-output CUDA recovery is now available only after a run was launched through the Windows supervisor; unsupervised runs, legacy database rows, CPU-only runs outside their existing worker enrollment, unrelated OS users, external GPU workloads and network filesystems are not covered. This recovers a process-tree quota lease; it does not estimate VRAM or authorize retrying a failed training/evaluation. N04 remains `PARTIAL`; next inspect another independent gap that does not require fresh market authorization or hosted CI.

## 2026-10-17 M3.1 standalone training lifecycle, hard-exit inference and wheel validation

- **Gap confirmed:** WFO candidates already register candidate recipe, seed, feature hash, and lifecycle events in the project registry, but direct `application.train` runs were absent. Failed standalone learner attempts and checkpoint resumes therefore could not contribute to the project's future trial inventory.
- **Change:** Standalone application training now registers a stable attempt identity from run ID, full training recipe, dataset/run fingerprint, feature hash, and seed. It appends `started` or `resumed`, then `completed`, `failed`, or `interrupted` with run/result evidence. When a resumed run's last event is still `started/resumed`, it appends `interrupted` with `resume_observed_without_terminal_event` before recording the new `resumed` event; this marks an incomplete prior invocation without claiming OS-level crash proof. WFO-owned training calls skip this path because WFO already records the candidate lifecycle. Project trial events now accept `completed`; append-only and idempotent event behavior is retained.

## 2026-10-17 A04 WFO preregistered query intents and scoped source read

- **Gap confirmed:** Freezing the curated marker before payload decoding prevented same-path source drift, but WFO still decoded a full snapshot before durable per-fold Test and final Holdout query identity existed. A crash/restart also needed stable intent identities without consuming a second query.
- **Change:** `curated_source_identity()` validates marker metadata and file identity without reading event payloads. WFO freezes that identity into the project development reservation, then reserves durable per-fold Test and final-Holdout query intents containing protocol, owner, market, split, exact half-open window, source hash, and candidate set. Intent IDs are idempotent for the same immutable identity; overlap and cross-owner development/evaluation reservations are blocked. All intents transition atomically to `started` before scoped payload decoding. WFO reads only the configured data envelope via `read_curated_window()`, verifies the semantic source hash, and later binds each scored candidate to its preregistered intent; the existing WFO evaluation ledger still controls when scoring starts. This distinguishes formal source-read authorization from metric consumption: Test/Holdout reads are durably marked started before decode, while evaluation remains phase-gated and one-shot.
- **Regression / verification:** `tests/test_curated_window_read.py tests/test_research_access.py tests/test_research_wfo.py tests/test_application_cli.py` → **112 passed** on Windows with all workspace package `src` directories in `PYTHONPATH`. Regressions cover metadata-before-payload order, immutable source drift rejection, idempotent intent reservation/start, candidate and window binding, cross-owner overlap blocking, and WFO resume/order. Ruff passed on the changed implementation/test files. Windows MyPy with workspace package sources in `MYPYPATH` passed for the changed reader, registry and WFO source files (4 source files).
- **Boundary / next cursor:** Tests used synthetic curated metadata, test fixtures and monkeypatched WFO data; no formal market payload, account, Validation, Test or Holdout payload was read, and no real WFO run was performed. The reader intentionally fails closed on row groups crossing a requested window boundary. WFO also requires the decoded scoped result hash to match the full frozen marker snapshot identity, so snapshots containing out-of-window events can be rejected; real-source compatibility remains unverified and must not be tested by opening unauthorized formal data. ## 2026-10-17 M2.2/A12 native pending quantity after partial fills

- **Gap reproduced:** An isolated synthetic Bar stress probe with `simulated_depth_fraction=0.5` caused a real Nautilus market order to remain `PARTIALLY_FILLED`. The next decision then raised `KeyError: 'leaves_qty'` in `HistoricalEnvironmentFactory.plan_with_rounding()`, because Nautilus `Order.to_dict()` contains total `quantity` and cumulative `filled_qty`, but does not provide the `leaves_qty` field the planner requires. The probe used only the test factory's synthetic fixture.
- **Change:** The native session adapter now validates pending order side and finite quantity facts, rejects negative or overfilled state, and derives `leaves_qty = quantity - filled_qty` from the native cumulative order state before exposing the pending-order DTO. The planner can therefore cancel/replan against the true remaining quantity after a partial fill.
- **Regression / verification:** Added `test_partial_native_fill_exposes_remaining_quantity_for_cancel_replace`: a synthetic native Engine under 50% Bar stress records `0.800` ordered, `0.400` filled, `0.400` remaining, then cancels the residual at the next decision while preserving the actual fill and canceled native order. The expanded `tests/test_historical_factory.py tests/test_research_metrics.py tests/test_native_liquidation_facts.py` suite passed **90 tests** (18 third-party warnings). Ruff passed for adapter/ledger/related tests; Windows MyPy passed for `session.py` and `native_metrics.py`; scoped `git diff --check` passed.

## 2026-10-17 N04 supervised GPU lease normal release guard

- **Gap found:** The enrolled GPU quota release guard required the named Job to contain exactly the current owner PID. A real Windows launch showed two active Job PIDs: the `python.exe` launcher parent and the actual Python owner process. This caused normal `ComputeAllocation.release()` to fail even after relinquishing the GPU lock. The prior guard also rejected on PID count before proving whether the shared GPU lock was still held.
- **Change:** `require_owned_job()` now obtains a native Toolhelp process-parent snapshot and reports the owner's ancestor PIDs. Enrolled GPU release first probes the shared kernel GPU lock; a still-held lock refuses refund. After the lock is free, release allows only the owner and its active ancestors in the exact memory-limited Job, so a live application descendant still prevents releasing quota.
- **Regression / verification:** Extended the real supervised Windows GPU lease test to exercise refusal while the GPU lock is held, successful normal release after unlocking, refusal when an application descendant remains active, then hard-exit recovery and reacquisition. With workspace package sources in `PYTHONPATH`, `pytest tests/test_resource_recovery.py tests/test_compute_device.py --basetemp .tmp/pytest-resource-all` → **27 passed**. Ruff passed for the two changed runtime files and recovery test; MyPy passed for both runtime files. A first isolated test attempt without `PYTHONPATH` could not import the workspace-only supervisor module; rerunning with the declared workspace sources passed. Pytest's default user temp location was not writable in the sandbox, so test runs use an in-workspace `--basetemp`.
- **Package check:** The first `uv build --package rles-compute-runtime` attempt could not write uv's sandbox interpreter cache. Retrying with `UV_CACHE_DIR=.tmp/uv-cache-n04-release` built the 0.4.1 sdist and wheel. Installed the wheel to `.tmp/n04-release-install` with `uv pip install --no-deps`; isolated imports of `resources` and `windows_recovery` resolved from that target.
- **Boundary / next cursor:** Windows process ancestry, quota safety and recovery only; no market, account, Validation, Test or Holdout payload was read. This does not add a VRAM cap or certify unrelated OS GPU processes. N04 remains `PARTIAL` on those platform/hardware limits. Continue with the highest-priority code-ready audit item; do not repeat the N04 hard-exit/CUDA probes without a relevant change.

## 2026-10-17 A02 negative terminal equity reaches feasibility screening

- **Gap found:** `ConstraintPriorityFitnessSpec.evaluate_episode()` delegated to the legacy scorer before checking insolvency. The legacy scorer rejects negative final equity, despite the constraint-first protocol defining `final_equity <= 0` as an infeasible bankruptcy. The installed native ES worker also rejected any negative terminal equity, so a finite bankruptcy fact could fail a worker lane instead of receiving an auditable rejection reason. Separately, the worker returned both endpoint equity and step log returns without checking their arithmetic consistency.
- **Change:** Constraint-priority v1/v2 now validate terminal equity is finite, clamp only the unused legacy economic-score input to zero, and classify the original terminal balance `<= 0` as `episode_insolvency_detected`. The legacy weighted protocol keeps its previous negative-equity rejection behavior. The native ES worker forwards finite negative terminal equity and explicit insolvency facts to the selected scoring protocol while still rejecting nonfinite values. For positive solvent episodes, it verifies `fsum(step_log_returns)` matches `log(final_equity) - log(initial_equity)` within `1e-8`; insolvent episodes bypass this log-return identity because the Gym reward uses its explicit insolvency penalty.
- **Regression / verification:** Added scorer tests for both constraint-priority versions and an installed-worker fixture that emits a finite `-5.0` terminal balance with native insolvency/liquidation facts, then emits inconsistent positive-endpoint facts and verifies rejection. With workspace package sources in `PYTHONPATH`: focused scorer/dispatcher/worker selection → **16 passed**; full `tests/test_compute_worker_pool.py` → **30 passed, 8 warnings** before the endpoint-parity guard; focused worker regression → **1 passed** after it; full `tests/test_native_training_episode.py` → **29 passed, 16 warnings** after it. Ruff passed on all changed implementation/tests; Windows MyPy passed for `fitness.py` and `native_es_worker.py`.
- **Boundary / next cursor:** This is deterministic scorer/worker contract validation only; no market, account, Validation, Test or Holdout payload was read. It proves a finite negative terminal balance is rejected as infeasible in the new protocol, not that the economic objective improves independent Validation. M1.2 remains `PARTIAL` pending its frozen independent Validation gate. Continue the next concrete code-ready audit gap; do not open Validation to extend this regression.

## 2026-10-17 M2.2 decision trace fee currency attribution

- **Change:** Added `_fee_summary()` to validate native commission amount/currency facts and aggregate `fees_by_currency`. Existing single-currency `fee` values are preserved and now accompanied by `fee_currency`; when several currencies occur, `fee` is `null` rather than a sum of unlike units. Non-submitted trace rows use the same explicit summary shape.

Execution-trace selection → **12 passed, 47 deselected**; full `tests/test_historical_factory.py` → **59 passed, 18 dependency warnings**; after adding the end-to-end currency assertion, that rollout test → **1 passed**. Ruff passed for `application.py` and `test_historical_factory.py`; Windows MyPy passed for `application.py` with all workspace package source roots in `MYPYPATH`.
- **Check corrections:** The first Ruff run caught an unused test import; it was removed. The first standalone MyPy invocation omitted `MYPYPATH`, treated installed workspace packages as untyped, and also exposed the new helper's invariant `list` annotation; the helper now accepts covariant `Sequence`, and the workspace-configured rerun passed. No test result from the incomplete MyPy invocation is counted as validation.
- **Boundary / next cursor:** Synthetic native-fill facts only; no market, account, Validation, Test, or Holdout payload was read. This closes the unit ambiguity in M2.2 trace aggregation, without adding multi-asset execution support or authenticating commission schedules. Continue from the next dependency-satisfied code gap; M0.1 trusted real cashflows, M0.2 hosted clean CI, M1.2 independent Validation, M1.3 compatible stage artifacts, M2.3 authentic depth, and M3.2 new independent market access remain bounded by their recorded external inputs.

## 2026-10-17 M2.2 per-decision policy-barrier equity attribution

- **Change:** `GymTradingEnv.step()` now emits the previous barrier equity, post-policy-barrier/pre-terminal equity, their exact Decimal difference and account currency after checking snapshot availability, finite values and stable currency. Terminal drain/flatten remains separately reported, and its `equity_before` must equal the last policy-barrier equity so terminal costs cannot be folded into the policy-step delta.

## 2026-10-17 A08 transfer archive duplicate-key rejection

- **Gap found:** Both raw Binance income import and paged-archive import used the default JSON decoder, which silently accepts duplicate object keys and keeps the last value. - **Regression / verification:** Added separate duplicate-key cases for a raw transfer payload and an archived page. `tests/test_binance_cashflows.py tests/test_research_metrics.py tests/test_native_liquidation_facts.py` → **70 passed**. ## 2026-10-17 N04 suspended primary-thread resume contract

- **Gap found:** `WindowsWorkerJob.run_suspended()` accepted every successful `ResumeThread()` return value. A suspended `CreateProcessW(CREATE_SUSPENDED)` primary thread should report a previous suspend count of exactly one. A return of zero means the thread was not suspended at resume time, so application code may have run before Job assignment enforced the memory ceiling; a value above one leaves it suspended and can hang indefinitely when no timeout is configured.
- **Change:** Added `_resume_suspended_primary_thread()` to accept only the expected count of one and fail closed for zero or unexpected positive counts. The existing supervisor `finally` path closes the Job on this exception, terminating any attached process tree.
- **Regression / verification:** Added cross-platform deterministic cases for expected count 1 and unexpected counts 0, 2, and 3. `$env:PYTHONPATH = (Get-ChildItem packages -Directory | ForEach-Object { Join-Path $_.FullName 'src' }) -join ';'; .venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q tests/test_windows_job_contract.py tests/test_windows_supervisor.py --basetemp .tmp/pytest-windows-resume-count-20261017-src --tb=short` → **12 passed** (including Windows Job memory-cap, descendant cleanup, and timeout integration cases). Ruff passed for `windows_job.py` and the new test; `mypy packages/compute_runtime/src/rles_compute_runtime/windows_job.py --platform win32` passed.
- **Boundary / next cursor:** Deterministic fake-kernel contract plus local Windows process-tree tests only; no market, account, Validation, Test, or Holdout data was read. This hardens the N04 launch precondition but does not prove Windows handle-failure behavior on other builds, Linux containment, VRAM limits, or cross-user isolation. Continue the next confirmed code-ready audit gap; retain external research and clean Hosted CI gates.

No dependency was installed into RouteA or the main project environment.

## 2026-10-21 A08 native account amount parsing parity

- **Gap found:** The metrics layer rejects malformed comma grouping, but `rles_gym_bridge.historical._money()` still removed commas while parsing native balance and margin amounts. A malformed value such as `1,2 USDT` could therefore become `12`, affecting account-state observations and risk inputs.
- **Change:** Removed separator deletion. The parser now accepts only a bare finite decimal or a two-token amount with the exact account currency; malformed grouping, extra tokens, non-finite values, and mismatched currency fail closed. Account total/free balance and initial/maintenance margin call sites now provide the frozen account currency. Added parser regressions in `tests/test_historical_factory.py`.
- **Verification:** RouteB isolated Python 3.12.10: `tests/test_historical_factory.py -k native_account_money` with a minimal Gymnasium import stub → **7 passed, 79 deselected**; no Gym environment was constructed. The parser accepted `1234.50000000 USDT` and bare `1234.5`, and rejected `1,2 USDT`, `1,234.50 USDT`, `12 USDC`, `NaN`, and extra tokens. `py_compile` passed for production/test files; Ruff passed for both files. The rest of `test_historical_factory.py` was not run because Gymnasium is absent from this isolated learner env; no package was installed into another project's environment.
- **Boundary / next cursor:** Deterministic native amount parsing only; no market/account history or formal split was read. M0.1/A08 remains `PARTIAL` pending source-authenticated complete external cashflows and a real-account long-window reconciliation. Continue the next independent source gap; keep Hosted CI, independent Validation, compatible stage artifacts, authentic depth, new independent-window access, and A09 under their existing gates.

## 2026-10-21 F7 streaming return statistics with exact tail risk

- **Gap found:** `AccountFactsAccumulator` already reduces account rows incrementally, but `series_metrics()` expanded all returns into a second Python list, allocated another full downside list, and sorted the entire path to compute exact VaR/CVaR. Peak memory therefore grew by multiple O(n) objects on long replays.
- **Change:** Rewrote `series_metrics()` as a one-pass reduction: Welford online mean/variance, scaled sum-of-squares for numeric stability, online compounded return/drawdown/downside statistics, and a bounded max-heap holding only the requested lower-tail count for exact VaR/CVaR. Added a one-pass-only Sequence regression and a finite large-dispersion guard.
- **Verification:** Isolated RouteB Python 3.12.10: research metrics plus numeric guards → **89 passed, 1 deselected**. The deselected case requires `nautilus_trader`, absent from the RouteB learner environment; the initial unfiltered command therefore reported that dependency-only failure. A dedicated one-pass regression passed, and 300 deterministic randomized comparisons against the prior `statistics`/full-sort reference matched within `2e-13` relative tolerance. Ruff and `py_compile` passed.
- **Boundary / next cursor:** Synthetic metric sequences only; no market/account or formal evaluation data was read. This closes avoidable O(n) return/downside/sort copies but does not remove the accumulator's one-scalar-per-period return vector, the factory's retained snapshot sequence, or authenticate economics. F7 is improved but remains partial pending end-to-end long-window RSS/trajectory evidence and snapshot streaming. Continue with the next independent A01–A14 code gap; external cashflows, Hosted CI, independent Validation, compatible stage artifacts, authentic depth, newly authorized independent windows, and A09 remain gated.
